Core Manager Interface (pkg/privilege/privilege.go)\\n\\n**Manager Interface** (lines 44-123):\\n- Central abstraction for privilege operations\\n- Key methods:\\n  - `RequestVerification(activeRole, db, table, column, priv)`: Checks user privilege at global, database, table, or column level\\n  - `RequestDynamicVerification(activeRoles, privName, grantable)`: Checks dynamic privileges (BACKUP_ADMIN, RESTORE_ADMIN, SYSTEM_USER, etc.)\\n  - `RequestVerificationWithUser()`: Verifies specific user privilege\\n  - `ConnectionVerification()`: Verifies privilege during connection establishment\\n  - `ActiveRoles()`, `GetAllRoles()`, `GetDefaultRoles()`: Role management\\n  - `FindEdge()`: Checks role graph edges\\n\\n**Context Binding Functions**:\\n- `BindPrivilegeManager()`: Attaches Manager to session context (lines 127-129)\\n- `GetPrivilegeManager()`: Retrieves Manager from context (lines 137-143)\\n\\n### 2. MySQLPrivilege Cache (pkg/privilege/privileges/cache.go)\\n\\n**MySQLPrivilege struct** (lines 286-300):\\nThe in-memory privilege cache implementing the core privilege lookup logic:\\n- Embedded `immutable` struct containing: user records, db records, tables_priv records, columns_priv records, default_roles, global_priv, dynamic privileges, and role graph\\n- Extended struct for accelerated lookups via maps\\n\\n**Role Graph Resolution** (lines 314-348):\\n- `FindAllRole()` (lines 314-337): Breadth-first search (BFS) algorithm to traverse role hierarchy and find all effective roles for a user\\n  -
